========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I think… almost all might be a little strong, maybe. I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is.

But the stuff I normally do look forward to — grilling on the weekend, fishing, even just putting on a record — lately I’ll see it there and I just kind of leave it.
